HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000131/B008","root_000131/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-8","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_00d5a14dbfc17ac72009","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:7:3\",\"root_000131\",[\"B008\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Bundan sonra sözü, hükmün ne kadar derine işlediğini ve inkârın artık yarar sağlamadığını da düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_000504/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:6","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0573c2723905f6ddb728","text":"6. **The *dīn* as ordered obedience inside a jurisdiction.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `د ي ن/B001` supplies obedience, worship, and the way or law followed; fixed `د ي ن/B006` supplies a city understood as organized authority and obedience. The focus window includes the bounded trustworthy city (95:3) and the supreme adjudicator (95:8). **Structural cue:** civic place, believing/action-bearing subjects, *dīn*, and judge occur in one compact progression. **Assigned role:** the city supplies jurisdiction, *dīn* the lived order, and the judge its authoritative determination. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies these social roles but not their constitution; I infer bounded community → norm-governed conduct → adjudication. **Reading change:** *al-dīn* reads simultaneously as a practiced order of submission and the law under which conduct is judged, not only as a future event.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_000504/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0be524cd27dc40eeb086","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:7:4\",\"root_000504\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hesaplaşma, güvenli bir topluluk içinde itaat edilen düzeni, yasayı ve bu düzenin yetkisini de kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000131/B002","root_001290/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1d36871c8f95cb95b1f9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:7:2\",\"root_001290\",[\"B002\"]],[\"95:7:3\",\"root_000131\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bundan sonra ifadesi, önceki yeminleri, yaratılışı, düşüşü ve ödülü birikmiş deliller hâline getirir; soru artık bilgi yokluğunu değil, bunca delile rağmen inkârı doğuran nedeni araştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_000504/B002","root_000504/B003","root_000504/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3107c4a1a107e1a2e540","text":"2. **Dīn as the whole obedience-debt-judgment order.** Lexical evidence: د ي ن B001, obedience/submission; د ي ن B002, reckoning/recompense; د ي ن B003, debt/credit; د ي ن B006, city of ordered authority; ء ج ر B001 in 95:6, recompense. Structural cue: the immediately preceding ayah joins عمل and أجر, while 95:8 names the judge. Assigned role: الدين gathers worshipful submission, debt/account, and ordered authority into one system. Abductive move: I infer that the packet activates multiple dīn branches simultaneously because the local context supplies work, recompense, city, and judge. Reading change: \"the dīn\" reads as an integrated order of obedience and requital, not only \"religion\" in isolation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_322f067ebc573f8204bd","text":"3. **Falsification by failing to carry the charge through.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ك ذ ب/B004` supplies an attack or undertaking that “lies” when it fails and proves true when carried through. Earlier `ق و م/B003` supplies rising with resolve to undertake a matter, while 95:6 supplies intentional work and sound action. **Structural cue:** the question follows the contrast between endowed formation, downward reversal, and those whose action matches assent. **Assigned role:** the charge image turns truth into performed completion; failure to enact the endowed capacity makes the claim “human in best setting” fail in practice. **Abductive move:** the packet does not call life a charge; I infer endowment → undertaking → enacted verification or failure from the creation/work contrast. **Reading change:** *yukadhdhibuka* can concern practical non-fulfilment, not speech alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000131/B002","root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3a29bfcb828c5b5bf4d5","text":"1. **A demand for the cause of post-evidence falsification.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ك ذ ب/B002` supplies declaring a person or report false, denying, or disproving it; fixed `ب ع د/B002` supplies temporal afterness. **Structural cue:** interrogative *mā* and initial *fa-* place the question after measured creation, downward return, exception, work, and enduring recompense (95:4–6). **Assigned role:** *baʿdu* turns the preceding sequence into an evidentiary threshold, while *mā* asks for the counter-cause that could still produce falsification. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies order but does not state that earlier claims compel assent; I infer evidence → expected confirmation, making denial causally anomalous. **Reading change:** the question is not a bare rebuke; it asks what mechanism could overturn the cumulative case “after” all this.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000131/B002","root_000504/B002","root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3cb002c70b9b01af91e2","text":"1. **Denial after staged evidence is an epistemic failure, not lack of data.** Lexical evidence: ك ذ ب B002, declaring someone or something false; ب ع د B002, afterness; د ي ن B002, reckoning/recompense. Structural cue: بعد follows a full sequence of oath signs, creation, fall, exception, and reward. Assigned role: بعد marks the lateness of denial; الدين names the reckoning/recompense now evidenced by the packet's own sequence. Abductive move: I infer that the question asks what active force remains capable of falsifying after evidence has accumulated. Reading change: the ayah reads as a diagnostic question about denial's mechanism, not simply a rebuke.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B003","root_001290/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_57b8c4088fbc77797474","text":"5. **A hidden wild-animal hesitation image.** Lexical evidence: ك ذ ب B007, wild animal runs then stops to look behind; ط و ر B006, wildness beyond domestic limit; ء ن س B003/B004, familiar human-facing side. Structural cue: the surah moves from human formation to return/denial; this reading is exploratory because the branch is narrow. Assigned role: the denier behaves like a creature that cannot remain in the human-facing, familiar order and keeps stopping/looking back. Abductive move: I infer an affective posture from the lexical image of halted wild flight. Reading change: denial becomes a recoil into wild hesitation after the human was made present and upright.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5f508b55e36ea7bb1cf8","text":"5. **The *dīn* as an outstanding account.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `د ي ن/B003` supplies debt, credit, borrowing, and deferred sale. It is activated by `ع م ل/B004` wage, `ء ج ر/B001` contract-like recompense, and the temporal force of fixed `ب ع د/B002`. **Structural cue:** labor and its promised return precede a question about what comes afterward, before adjudication closes the sequence. **Assigned role:** debt supplies deferred obligation, *after* supplies maturity, and judgment supplies settlement. **Abductive move:** the packet does not posit a literal financial ledger; I infer deed/benefit → outstanding due → later settlement as an economic analogy. **Reading change:** *dīn* can be heard as a due account whose postponement does not make it unreal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B004","root_001290/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_70fc38f29ba3750d9ba5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:7:2\",\"root_001290\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yalanlama, yalnız sözle reddetmek değil, üstlenilen işi sonuna kadar götüremeyip iddiayı davranışta boşa çıkarmaktır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B004","root_001290/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_815b319c413671b447af","text":"4. **The denier fails to carry through the charge.** Lexical evidence: ك ذ ب B004, whether a charge proves true by being carried through or fails; ك ذ ب B005, not delaying to act; ق و م B003, resolve/rising to undertake; ع م ل B001, intentional action. Structural cue: belief/work in 95:6 is followed by denial in 95:7. Assigned role: denial is not just a false statement; it is a failed charge or failed enactment when measured against the work that would prove assent. Abductive move: I infer that speech-act and action are tested together: a claim is true only if carried through. Reading change: \"what makes you deny\" can mean what makes your charge/action fail after the evidence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B002","root_000504/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_86c2cce08e6c634580b2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:7:4\",\"root_000504\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hesaplaşma, yerine getirilen davranışların karşılığını bekleyen, vadesi gelince kapatılacak bir borç hesabı niteliği de taşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8a1eb86a6541fa54e7d9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:7:2\",\"root_001290\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Soru, aynı zamanda “hesap konusunda seni kim yalancı çıkarabilir?” biçiminde bir güvenilirlik sınamasına da açılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-9","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8c4696e56eb70271b01b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:7:2\",\"root_001290\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Koşup durarak arkasına bakan yabani hayvan görüntüsü, inkârı delillerden sonra insan düzeninden geriye sıçrayan kararsızlık gibi tasvir eder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000131/B001","root_000131/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_95f20848c541e4c2019f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:7:3\",\"root_000131\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bundan sonra sözü, zamansal sonralığın yanında hakikatten uzaklaşma ve yıkıcı dışlanma mesafesi de kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B002","root_000504/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9abe3a3a42ac804c3493","text":"4. **The *dīn* as reckoning and recompense.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `د ي ن/B002` supplies judgment, accounting, recompense, and requital for deeds. **Structural cue:** *ajr* for work immediately precedes the question, and repeated `ح ك م/B002` adjudication immediately follows it in 95:8. **Assigned role:** *dīn* names the accounting operation that connects performed deeds to assigned return; the final judge guarantees its execution. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the work/reward and judge endpoints; I infer that *dīn* is their mediating reckoning. **Reading change:** “the *dīn*” becomes specifically the reality of accountable requital, while a broader religious-way reading remains available.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b6faf9a26c7d37f72bef","text":"95:8 was the sole later ayah and the decisive surprise. Its doubled `ح ك م/B002` strongly selected reckoning/recompense in fixed `د ي ن/B002`, while `ح ك م/B003` made the preceding falsification question forensic: whether the issue is induced denial or an attempt to discredit the addressee, a truth-reaching judge now controls the outcome.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b81dccd7017ccd31cde2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:7:4\",\"root_000504\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Hesap düzeni, kişinin kendini üstün görmesini kırıp onu sonuçlarına boyun eğdiren bir kuvvet taşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e262cf05413a769e49fa","text":"95:8 immediately answered 95:7, so the retrospective surprise is internal to the two-ayah closure: denial is not met by more evidence but by the identity and competence of the judge. Looking back to 95:1-6, بعد became heavier than temporal \"after\"; it now means after fruits/provision, places, secure trust, measured creation, fall, exception, and reward. The question exposes denial as a remaining force after the packet has exhausted material, spatial, anthropological, and legal evidence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000131/B001","root_000131/B004","root_000131/B008","root_000131/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f2048f045a6b38b5dd0b","text":"3. **After-distance can be depth of judgment or ruinous remoteness.** Lexical evidence: ب ع د B001, remoteness; ب ع د B004, being far off as ruin/curse; ب ع د B008, depth of judgment; ب ع د B009, no benefit/worth. Structural cue: بعد sits between the denial verb and الدين. Assigned role: \"after\" is temporal on the surface, but the branch inventory permits distance from nearness, ruinous exclusion, or far-reaching judgment. Abductive move: I infer that denial after evidence creates not only chronological lateness but moral distance from the secure/merciful frame. Reading change: the ayah can ask what sends you far away from judgment's obviousness after all this.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f5f1b634d7a4f46b236e","text":"2. **A coexisting credibility trial.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ك ذ ب/B002` explicitly includes attributing falsehood to a person as well as to a report. **Structural cue:** the surface form carries the second-person object suffix *-ka*, and the next ayah introduces Allah and the judges. **Assigned role:** the addressee can occupy the position of the one being declared false, with *al-dīn* naming the matter on which credibility is tested. **Abductive move:** the packet does not identify an accuser; I infer a forensic alternative in which the question asks what could discredit “you” regarding the reckoning before the final judge is named. **Reading change:** without cancelling induced-denial syntax, the ayah can stage a trial of the addressee’s truthfulness rather than only describe the addressee’s act of denial.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
